## 25. sb_rowT_tally_b758ff420f8f0d44c8996020

Question: Count the coat(s) and heater(s) and paper(s) in this room together. How many are there in total?

```text

TALLY coat: frame 8: 1 (middle: 1) -> 1.
TALLY heater: frame 2: 1 (left: 1); frame 3: 1 (middle: 1) -> 2.
TALLY paper: frame 10: 3 (left: 3); frame 12: 3 (middle: 1, right: 2) -> 6.
Total: 1 + 2 + 6 = 9.
</think>

9
```
